Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the quantum_mechanics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 122. The moduli space has dimension at most 659 with 3 connected components.

This completes the proof. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the recursion lemma fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
